import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { StorefrontCMSPanel } from "../components/admin/StoreFrontCmsPanel";
import {
  Lock,
  ShieldAlert,
  MapPin,
  ExternalLink,
  MessageCircle,
  Save,
  CheckCircle2,
  Truck,
  Sparkles,
  Package,
  Printer,
  Copy,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  Activity,
  Trash2,
  Eye,
  Key,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useStore } from "../context/StoreContext";
import { BUSINESS_CONFIG } from "../constants";
import { OrderStatus, Order } from "../types";

export const AdminPage: React.FC = () => {
  const {
    currentUser,
    isAdminAuthenticated,
    authenticateAdmin,
    logoutAdmin,
    cmsConfig,
    orders,
    adminOrders,
    deleteOrder,
    updateOrderStatus,
    updateOrderDetails,
    showToast,
    themeMode,
    togglePreviewStorefront,
  } = useStore();

  const isDark = themeMode === "dark";

  const [pinInput, setPinInput] = useState("");
  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState(false);
  const [showCloudflareGuide, setShowCloudflareGuide] = useState(false);

  // Delhivery Logistics & Testing State
  const [checkingPincode, setCheckingPincode] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [pincodeStatus, setPincodeStatus] = useState<
    Record<
      string,
      { serviceable: boolean; cod: boolean; prepaid: boolean; message?: string }
    >
  >({});
  const [manifesting, setManifesting] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [manifestError, setManifestError] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [customAwb, setCustomAwb] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [copiedPorter, setCopiedPorter] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Bangalore Local Courier Manual Tracking State
  const [blrCourier, setBlrCourier] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [blrNotes, setBlrNotes] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [blrTracking, setBlrTracking] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [blrStatus, setBlrStatus] = useState<Record<string, OrderStatus>>({});
  const [savedSuccessRef, setSavedSuccessRef] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Delhivery Diagnostic State
  const [pingingApi, setPingingApi] = useState(false);
  const [pingResult, setPingResult] = useState<{
    status: string;
    authenticated: boolean;
    tokenMasked: string;
    warehouse: string;
    originPincode: string;
    sampleWaybill?: string;
    latencyMs?: number;
    message?: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Automatic background carrier status sync for active Delhivery orders
  useEffect(() => {
    const activeDelhiveryOrders = orders.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.trackingNumber &&
        !o.trackingNumber.startsWith("MV-BLR") &&
        o.orderStatus !== "Delivered" &&
        o.orderStatus !== "Cancelled"
    );

    if (activeDelhiveryOrders.length === 0) return;

    const syncActiveShipments = async () => {
      for (const ord of activeDelhiveryOrders) {
        try {
          const res = await fetch(`/api?action=delhivery_track&waybill=${ord.trackingNumber}`);
          const data = await res.json();
          const scanStatus = data?.data?.ShipmentData?.[0]?.Shipment?.Status?.Status;

          if (scanStatus) {
            const mapped = mapDelhiveryToWebStatus(scanStatus);
            if (mapped && mapped !== ord.orderStatus) {
              await updateOrderStatus(ord.id || ord.orderRef, mapped);
            }
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn(`Background sync skipped for ${ord.trackingNumber}:`, e);
        }
      }
    };

    // Run automatically when dashboard opens
    syncActiveShipments();

    // Helper to map live Delhivery carrier status strings to MANOPAVANA website order stages
    const mapDelhiveryToWebStatus = (delhiveryStatus: string): "Placed" | "Packed" | "Dispatched" | "Delivered" | "Cancelled" | null => {
      const s = String(delhiveryStatus || "").toLowerCase();

      if (s.includes("delivered")) {
        return "Delivered";
      }
      if (
        s.includes("in transit") ||
        s.includes("dispatched") ||
        s.includes("out for delivery") ||
        s.includes("in-transit")
      ) {
        return "Dispatched";
      }
      if (
        s.includes("manifest") ||
        s.includes("ready to ship") ||
        s.includes("scheduled") ||
        s.includes("open")
      ) {
        return "Packed";
      }
      if (s.includes("cancel")) {
        return "Cancelled";
      }

      return null;
    };

    // Re-check automatically every 5 minutes while dashboard remains open
    const interval = setInterval(syncActiveShipments, 5 * 60 * 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [orders]);

  const facilityAddress =
    cmsConfig?.facilityAddress || BUSINESS_CONFIG.FACILITY_ADDRESS;

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const ok = await authenticateAdmin(pinInput.trim());
    if (!ok) {
      setPinError(true);
    } else {
      setPinError(false);
      showToast("Administrator portal unlocked successfully!");
    }
  };

  const handlePingDelhivery = async () => {
    setPingingApi(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api?action=delhivery_test_connection");
      const data = await res.json();
      setPingResult(data);
      if (data.status === "SUCCESS") {
        showToast(`Delhivery API connected! Latency: ${data.latencyMs}ms`);
      } else {
        showToast(data.message || "Delhivery connection check failed");
      }
    } catch {
      showToast("Error connecting to Delhivery API");
    } finally {
      setPingingApi(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveBlrTracking = async (orderRef: string, currentOrd: Order) => {
    try {
      const courierVal =
        blrCourier[orderRef] !== undefined
          ? blrCourier[orderRef]
          : currentOrd.courier || "MANOPAVANA Peenya Direct Fleet";
      const notesVal =
        blrNotes[orderRef] !== undefined
          ? blrNotes[orderRef]
          : currentOrd.notes || "";
      const trackingVal =
        blrTracking[orderRef] !== undefined
          ? blrTracking[orderRef]
          : currentOrd.trackingNumber || "";
      const statusVal =
        blrStatus[orderRef] || currentOrd.orderStatus || "Dispatched";

      await updateOrderDetails(orderRef, {
        courier: courierVal,
        notes: notesVal,
        trackingNumber: trackingVal,
        orderStatus: statusVal,
      });

      setSavedSuccessRef((p) => ({ ...p, [orderRef]: true }));
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSavedSuccessRef((p) => ({ ...p, [orderRef]: false }));
      }, 4000);
      showToast(`Bengaluru tracking details saved for #${orderRef}`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Failed to save tracking:", err);
      showToast(`Error saving tracking details for #${orderRef}`);
    }
  };

  const handleCopyRiderDrop = (ord: Order) => {
    const addr = (ord?.shippingAddress || {}) as any;
    const mapsLink =
      addr.lat && addr.lng
        ? `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${addr.lat},${addr.lng}`
        : `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(
          `${addr.addressLine1 || ""}, Bengaluru - ${addr.pincode || ""}`
        )}`;
    const text = `ðŸ“¦ MANOPAVANA DROP DETAILS\nOrder: #${ord.orderRef}\nCustomer: ${addr.fullName || "Customer"
      }\nPhone: ${addr.phone || "N/A"}\nAddress: ${addr.addressLine1 || ""}${addr.landmark ? ` (Near ${addr.landmark})` : ""
      }, ${addr.district || "Bengaluru"} - ${addr.pincode || ""}\nPayment: ${ord.paymentMethod?.toUpperCase()} (â‚¹${ord.total
      })\nLocation Pin: ${mapsLink}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    showToast("ðŸ“‹ Drop address & GPS copied for delivery rider!");
  };

  const handleCheckPincode = async (orderRef: string, pincode?: string) => {
    if (!pincode) {
      showToast("No valid pincode provided");
      return;
    }
    setCheckingPincode((p) => ({ ...p, [orderRef]: true }));
    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        `/api?action=delhivery_check_pincode&pincode=${encodeURIComponent(pincode)}`
      );
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.status === "SUCCESS") {
        setPincodeStatus((p) => ({
          ...p,
          [orderRef]: {
            serviceable: Boolean(data.serviceable),
            cod: Boolean(data.cod),
            prepaid: Boolean(data.prepaid),
            message: data.serviceable
              ? `Serviceable (${data.city || ""}, ${data.state || ""})`
              : "Not Serviceable",
          },
        }));
        showToast(
          data.serviceable
            ? `Delhivery delivers to ${pincode}! (COD: ${data.cod ? "Yes" : "No"})`
            : `Pincode ${pincode} not serviceable by Delhivery`
        );
      } else {
        showToast("Pincode check failed");
      }
    } catch {
      showToast("Network error checking Delhivery pincode");
    } finally {
      setCheckingPincode((p) => ({ ...p, [orderRef]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleManifestDelhivery = async (order: Order) => {
    setManifesting((p) => ({ ...p, [order.orderRef]: true }));
    setManifestError((p) => ({ ...p, [order.orderRef]: "" }));

    const addr = (order?.shippingAddress || {}) as any;
    const rawItems = order?.items;
    const items: any[] = Array.isArray(rawItems)
      ? rawItems
      : rawItems && typeof rawItems === "object"
        ? Object.values(rawItems)
        : [];

    const totalGrams =
      items.reduce((acc: number, item: any) => {
        const variant = String(item?.variant || "");
        const isLarge = variant.includes("1L") || variant.includes("3L");
        const gramsPerUnit = isLarge ? 1000 : 550;
        return acc + gramsPerUnit * (Number(item?.quantity) || 1);
      }, 0) || 1000;

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api?action=delhivery_create_shipment`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          orderRef: order.orderRef,
          customerName: addr.fullName || "Customer",
          phone: addr.phone || "",
          address: `${addr.addressLine1 || ""}${addr.landmark ? `, Near ${addr.landmark}` : ""
            }`,
          pincode: addr.pincode || "560058",
          city: addr.city || addr.district || "Bengaluru",
          state: addr.state || "Karnataka",
          paymentMode: order.paymentMethod === "cod" ? "COD" : "Prepaid",
          totalAmount: order.total || 0,
          weightGrams: totalGrams,
          productsDesc:
            items.map((i: any) => `${i.title} (${i.variant})`).join(", ") ||
            "Wood-pressed Cooking Oil",
          quantity: items.reduce((acc: number, i: any) => acc + (Number(i?.quantity) || 1), 0),
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.status === "SUCCESS" && data.waybill) {
        await updateOrderDetails(order.orderRef, {
          trackingNumber: data.waybill,
          courier: "Delhivery Express",
          orderStatus: "Dispatched",
        });
        showToast(`Delhivery Shipment Manifested! AWB: #${data.waybill}`);
      } else {
        const errMsg =
          data.message ||
          "Failed to generate waybill. Please verify pickup facility name or Delhivery balance.";
        setManifestError((p) => ({
          ...p,
          [order.orderRef]: errMsg,
        }));
        showToast(`Manifest Error: ${errMsg}`);
      }
    } catch {
      setManifestError((p) => ({
        ...p,
        [order.orderRef]: "Network error connecting to Delhivery API",
      }));
      showToast("Error contacting Delhivery API");
    } finally {
      setManifesting((p) => ({ ...p, [order.orderRef]: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleSaveCustomAwb = async (orderRef: string) => {
    const awb = (customAwb[orderRef] || "").trim();
    if (!awb) {
      showToast("Please enter a valid AWB number");
      return;
    }
    await updateOrderDetails(orderRef, {
      trackingNumber: awb,
      courier: "Delhivery Express",
      orderStatus: "Dispatched",
    });
    setCustomAwb((p) => ({ ...p, [orderRef]: "" }));
    showToast(`Saved Delhivery AWB #${awb}`);
  };

  const handleStatusChange = async (ord: Order, newStatus: OrderStatus) => {
    if (newStatus === "Cancelled" && ord.trackingNumber && !ord.trackingNumber.startsWith("MV-BLR")) {
      try {
        showToast("Notifying Delhivery of order cancellation...");
        const res = await fetch("/api?action=delhivery_cancel_order", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ waybill: ord.trackingNumber }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === "SUCCESS") {
          showToast("Delhivery shipment cancelled & wallet credited!");
        } else {
          showToast(`Delhivery notice: ${data.message || "Failed to cancel on carrier"}`);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Delhivery cancellation sync notice:", e);
      }
    }

    await updateOrderStatus(ord.id || ord.orderRef, newStatus);
  };

  const handleCopyPorter = (order: Order) => {
    const addr = (order?.shippingAddress || {}) as any;
    const rawItems = order?.items;
    const items: any[] = Array.isArray(rawItems)
      ? rawItems
      : rawItems && typeof rawItems === "object"
        ? Object.values(rawItems)
        : [];

    const text = `Customer: ${addr.fullName || "Customer"}
Phone: ${addr.phone || "N/A"}
Address: ${addr.addressLine1 || ""}${addr.landmark ? `, Near ${addr.landmark}` : ""
      }, ${addr.district || addr.city || "Bengaluru"} - ${addr.pincode || ""}
Items: ${items.map((i: any) => `${i.title} (${i.variant}) x${i.quantity}`).join(", ")}
Order Ref: #${order.orderRef}
Total: â‚¹${order.total} (${order.paymentMethod?.toUpperCase()})`;

    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedPorter((p) => ({ ...p, [order.orderRef]: true }));
    showToast("Copied customer drop details for Porter app!");
    setTimeout(() => {
      setCopiedPorter((p) => ({ ...p, [order.orderRef]: false }));
    }, 2500);
  };

  const displayOrders =
    (adminOrders && adminOrders.length > 0 ? adminOrders : orders) || [];

  if (!isAdminAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <div
        className={`w-full min-h-[calc(100vh-80px)] py-20 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors px-4 box-border ${isDark ? "bg-[#140D09] text-[#FAF4EA]" : "bg-[#FAF7F2] text-[#24140A]"
          }`}
      >
        <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto text-center">
          <div
            className={`p-8 rounded-3xl border shadow-xl space-y-6 ${isDark
              ? "bg-[#1C120B] border-[#362114]"
              : "bg-white border-[#E8DEC8]"
              }`}
          >
            <div
              className={`w-16 h-16 mx-auto rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center ${isDark
                ? "bg-[#150D07] border-[#362114]"
                : "bg-[#FAF6EE] border-[#DDD1BE]"
                }`}
            >
              <Lock className="w-8 h-8 text-[#E08F35]" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h1
                className={`text-xl font-bold font-serif ${isDark ? "text-[#FAF4EA]" : "text-[#1F120A]"
                  }`}
              >
                MANOPAVANA Administrator Gate
              </h1>
              <p
                className={`text-xs ${isDark ? "text-[#D8C8B8]" : "text-[#5C4230]"
                  }`}
              >
                {currentUser?.email ? (
                  <>
                    Logged in as{" "}
                    <span className="font-semibold text-amber-500">
                      {currentUser.email}
                    </span>
                    . Enter PIN to unlock live CMS & dispatch hub.
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>Enter Master Security PIN to unlock store management.</>
                )}
              </p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  maxLength={6}
                  placeholder="Enter Secret Admin PIN"
                  value={pinInput}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setPinInput(e.target.value);
                    setPinError(false);
                  }}
                  className={`w-full text-center tracking-widest text-lg font-mono font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#E08F35]/40 transition-colors ${isDark
                    ? "bg-[#150D07] border border-[#362114] text-[#FAF4EA] placeholder-[#8A7665]"
                    : "bg-[#FAF7F2] border border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#1F120A] placeholder-[#8A7665]"
                    }`}
                />
                {pinError && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-500 font-semibold mt-1.5">
                    Invalid PIN. Please enter your secret admin PIN.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full py-3 rounded-xl bg-[#5E3210] hover:bg-[#43230A] text-white font-bold text-xs shadow-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
              >
                Unlock Administration Portal
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowCloudflareGuide(true)}
                className={`w-full py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-semibold border flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${isDark
                  ? "bg-transparent border-[#362114] text-[#D8C8B8] hover:bg-[#1C120B]"
                  : "bg-transparent border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#5C4230] hover:bg-[#FAF6EE]"
                  }`}
              >
                <Key className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                <span>How to store PIN encrypted in Cloudflare</span>
              </button>
            </form>

            <p
              className={`text-[11px] ${isDark ? "text-[#A89482]" : "text-[#7A5E48]"
                }`}
            >
              Authorized Facility: Peenya 2nd Stage, Bangalore â€¢ Edge Verified
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {showCloudflareGuide && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4">

                                  <Save className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />
                                  <span>Save Tracking Details</span>
                                </button>

                                {savedSuccessRef[ord.orderRef] && (
                                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 animate-fade-in">
                                    <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                                    <span>Saved to Firestore!</span>
                                  </span>
                                )}

                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleCopyRiderDrop(ord)}
                                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${isDark
                                    ? "bg-[#1E130B] border-[#3D2514] text-[#FAF4EA] hover:bg-[#28180E]"
                                    : "bg-white border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#5E3210] hover:bg-[#FAF6EE]"
                                    }`}
                                >
                                  <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                                  <span>Copy Rider Drop Details</span>
                                </button>

                                <a
                                  href="https://borzodelivery.com/in"
                                  target="_blank"
                                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                                  onClick={() => handleCopyRiderDrop(ord)}
                                  className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
                                >
                                  <Truck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                  <span>Book on Borzo</span>
                                  <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 opacity-80" />
                                </a>
                              </div>

                              <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
                                <span>Live on Website Order Tracker</span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <div
                            className={`p-3 rounded-xl border space-y-2.5 text-xs ${isDark
                              ? "bg-[#191009] border-[#3D2514]"
                              : "bg-[#F7F2E8] border-[#DFD4BF]"
                              }`}
                          >
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-bold">
                                <Truck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                                <span
                                  className={
                                    isDark ? "text-[#FAF4EA]" : "text-[#1F120A]"
                                  }
                                >
                                  Delhivery Logistics Hub (Outside Bengaluru)
                                </span>
                              </div>

                              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                {pincodeStatus[ord.orderRef] ? (
                                  <span
                                    className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${pincodeStatus[ord.orderRef].serviceable
                                      ? "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 border border-emerald-500/30"
                                      : "bg-red-500/15 text-red-600 border border-red-500/30"
                                      }`}
                                  >
                                    {pincodeStatus[ord.orderRef].serviceable
                                      ? `Delhivery Deliverable (COD: ${pincodeStatus[ord.orderRef].cod
                                        ? "Yes"
                                        : "No"
                                      })`
                                      : "Delhivery Not Serviceable"}
                                  </span>
                                ) : (
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() =>
                                      handleCheckPincode(
                                        ord.orderRef,
                                        addr.pincode
                                      )
                                    }
                                    disabled={checkingPincode[ord.orderRef]}
                                    className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${isDark
                                      ? "bg-[#1C120B] border-[#3D2514] text-[#E08F35] hover:bg-[#28180E]"
                                      : "bg-white border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#5E3210] hover:bg-[#FAF6EE]"
                                      }`}
                                  >
                                    {checkingPincode[ord.orderRef]
                                      ? "Checking..."
                                      : `Verify Pin ${addr.pincode || ""}`}
                                  </button>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            {ord.trackingNumber ? (
                              <div
                                className={`p-2.5 rounded-lg border space-y-2 ${isDark
                                  ? "bg-[#140D07] border-[#362114]"
                                  : "bg-white border-[#E5DAC8]"
                                  }`}

                                      Shipment Booked (
                                      {ord.courier || "Delhivery Express"})
                                    </span>
                                    <span
                                      className={`font-mono text-xs font-bold ${isDark
                                        ? "text-[#FAF4EA]"
                                        : "text-[#1F120A]"
                                        }`}
                                    >
                                      AWB: {ord.trackingNumber}
                                    </span>
                                  </div>

                                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <a
                                      href={`/api?action=delhivery_packing_slip&waybill=${encodeURIComponent(
                                        ord.trackingNumber
                                      )}`}
                                      target="_blank"
                                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                                      className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors ${isDark
                                        ? "bg-[#1E130B] border-[#3D2514] text-[#FAF4EA] hover:bg-[#2A1A0F]"
                                        : "bg-[#FAF6EE] border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#1F120A] hover:bg-[#EFE8DC]"
                                        }`}
                                      title="Print Delhivery Packing Slip"
                                    >
                                      <Printer className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                                      <span>Print Label</span>
                                    </a>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            ) : (
                              <div className="space-y-2">
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                  <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => handleManifestDelhivery(ord)}
                                    disabled={manifesting[ord.orderRef]}
                                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#5E3210] hover:bg-[#43230A] disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"

                                    <Truck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-300" />

                                        ? "Generating Waybill..."

                                    </span>

                                    <input
                                      type="text"
                                      placeholder="Or paste AWB from Delhivery One..."
                                      value={customAwb[ord.orderRef] || ""}
                                      onChange={(e) =>
                                        setCustomAwb((p) => ({
                                          ...p,
                                          [ord.orderRef]: e.target.value,
                                        }))
                                      }
                                      className={`flex-1 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-lg font-mono focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#E08F35] ${isDark
                                        ? "bg-[#140D07] border border-[#362114] text-[#FAF4EA] placeholder-[#8A7665]"
                                        : "bg-white border border-[#DDD1BE] text-[#1F120A] placeholder-[#8A7665]"
                                        }`}
                                    />
                                    <button
                                      type="button"
                                      onClick={() =>
                                        handleSaveCustomAwb(ord.orderRef)
                                      }
                                      className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-stone-700 hover:bg-stone-800 text-white text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer transition-colors shrink-0"
                                    >
                                      Save AWB
                                    </button>
                                  </div>
                                </div>

                                {manifestError[ord.orderRef] && (
                                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 text-[11px] space-y-1">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold">
                                      <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                      <span>Delhivery Manifest Error:</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p className="opacity-90">
                                      {manifestError[ord.orderRef]}
                                    </p>
                                  </div>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Customer Actions */}
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                            <a
                              href={mapsUrl}
                              target="_blank"
                              rel="noopener noreferrer"
                              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${isDark
                                ? "bg-[#1C120B] border-[#362114] text-amber-200 hover:border-[#E08F35]"
                                : "bg-white border-[#DDD1BE] hover:border-[#E08F35] text-[#5E3210]"
                                }`}
                            >
                              <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                              <span>Google Maps Pin</span>
                              <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 text-[#A69788]" />
                            </a>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleCopyPorter(ord)}
                              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${copiedPorter[ord.orderRef]
                                ? "bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-600"
                                : isDark
                                  ? "bg-[#1C120B] border-[#362114] text-[#FAF4EA] hover:border-[#E08F35]"
                                  : "bg-white border-[#DDD1BE] hover:border-[#E08F35] text-[#1F120A]"
                                }`}
                            >
                              {copiedPorter[ord.orderRef] ? (
                                <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                              ) : (
                                <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#E08F35]" />
                              )}
                              <span>
                                {copiedPorter[ord.orderRef]
                                  ? "Copied for Porter!"
                                  : "Copy for Porter"}
                              </span>
                            </button>
                          </div>

                          <a
                            href={
                              ord.trackingNumber
                                ? `https://wa.me/91${cleanPhone}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
                                  `Hi ${addr.fullName || "Customer"
                                  }, your MANOPAVANA order #${ord.orderRef
                                  } has been dispatched! Track your parcel live here: https://www.delhivery.com/track/package/${ord.trackingNumber
                                  }`
                                )}`
                                : waUrl
                            }
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-2xs"
                          >
                            <MessageCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>
                              {ord.trackingNumber
                                ? "WhatsApp Tracking"
                                : "WhatsApp Dispatch"}
                            </span>
                          </a>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

                    ? "text-[#FAF4EA] border-[#2D1B10]"
                    : "text-[#23150C] border-[#EFE8DC]"
                }`}
              >
                Order Summary
              </h2>

              <div className="space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                <div
                  className={`flex items-center justify-between ${
                    isDark ? "text-[#D6C4B0]" : "text-[#5C402D]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>Items Subtotal</span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-bold ${
                      isDark ? "text-[#FAF4EA]" : "text-[#23150C]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    â‚¹{subtotal}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {loyalty.isActive && (
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-emerald-600 font-medium">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" />
                      <span>Bottle Loyalty Discount (10%)</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-bold">-â‚¹{discount}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div
                  className={`flex items-center justify-between ${
                    isDark ? "text-[#D6C4B0]" : "text-[#5C402D]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <span>Bangalore Delivery Fee</span>
                    {shippingFee === 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-700 font-bold bg-emerald-50 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        FREE
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span
                    className={`font-bold ${
                      isDark ? "text-[#FAF4EA]" : "text-[#23150C]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {shippingFee === 0 ? "â‚¹0" : `â‚¹${shippingFee}`}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div
                  className={`pt-3 border-t flex items-center justify-between text-sm ${
                    isDark ? "border-[#2D1B10]" : "border-[#EFE8DC]"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className={`font-bold ${
                      isDark ? "text-[#FAF4EA]" : "text-[#23150C]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    Total Amount
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className={`text-2xl font-black font-serif ${
                      isDark ? "text-amber-300" : "text-[#C8751B]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    â‚¹{total}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <p
                  className={`text-[11px] text-right ${
                    isDark ? "text-[#A8907E]" : "text-[#7A5E48]"
                  }`}
                >
                  Inclusive of all taxes & GST (GSTIN: {BUSINESS_CONFIG.GSTIN})
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleProceedToCheckout}
                className="w-full py-4 rounded-xl bg-[#5E3210] hover:bg-[#43230A] text-white font-black text-xs shadow-md transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer hover:brightness-110"
              >
                {!currentUser ? (
                  <>
                    <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
                    <span>Sign In to Proceed to Checkout</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span>Proceed to Delivery & Payment</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300" />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>

              <div
                className={`pt-2 text-[11px] space-y-1.5 ${
                  isDark ? "text-[#D6C4B0]" : "text-[#5C402D]"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  <span>Dispatched directly from Peenya 2nd Stage, Bangalore</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  <span>100% Secure Online Payment (UPI, Cards, NetBanking) & Cash on Delivery</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
