import emailjs from "@emailjs/browser";
import { EMAILJS_CONFIG } from "../constants";
import { Order } from "../types";

/**
 * Dispatch an order confirmation email via EmailJS (service_c6el9r8, template_zcdzrbl, SzEt0unJ8krw0qHVt)
 * to manopavana@gmail.com
 */
export async function sendOrderConfirmationEmail(order: Order) {
  try {
    // 1. Build a clean, multi-line items breakdown
    const itemsList =
      order.items && order.items.length > 0
        ? order.items.map(
            (item, idx) =>
              `${idx + 1}. ${item.title} (${item.variant}) x ${item.quantity} = â‚¹${item.price * item.quantity}`
          )
        : ["1. Artisanal Wood-Pressed Oil Order"];

    const itemsSummary = itemsList.join("\n");

    // 2. Format customer shipping details
    const customerName =
      order.shippingAddress?.fullName?.trim() || "Valued Customer";
    const customerPhone =
      order.shippingAddress?.phone?.trim() || "N/A";
    const customerEmail =
      order.shippingAddress?.email?.trim() || "N/A";
    const addressLine = order.shippingAddress?.addressLine1 || "";
    const district = order.shippingAddress?.district || order.shippingAddress?.city || "Bengaluru";
    const state = order.shippingAddress?.state || "Karnataka";
    const pincode = order.shippingAddress?.pincode || "";
    const deliveryAddress = [addressLine, district, `${state} - ${pincode}`]
      .filter((part) => part && part.trim() !== "-")
      .join(", ");
    const landmark = order.shippingAddress?.landmark || "N/A";

    const isCod = order.paymentMethod === "cod" || order.paymentMode === "COD";
    const paymentMethodLabel = isCod
      ? "Cash on Delivery (COD)"
      : "Online Payment (Completed)";

    const mapsLink =
      order.shippingAddress?.lat && order.shippingAddress?.lng
        ? `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${order.shippingAddress.lat},${order.shippingAddress.lng}`
        : "https://www.google.com/maps?q=12.9716,77.5946";

    const formattedDate =
      order.date && order.date !== "Just now"
        ? order.date
        : new Date().toLocaleString("en-IN", { timeZone: "Asia/Kolkata" });

    // Comprehensive payload containing all standard naming conventions
    // ensures ANY EmailJS template placeholder is populated without blanks
    const fullOrderText = [
      `ðŸ›’ NEW ORDER #${order.orderRef}`,
      `ðŸ“… Date: ${formattedDate}`,
      `ðŸ’³ Payment: ${paymentMethodLabel}`,
      `ðŸ’° Total Amount: â‚¹${order.total} (Subtotal: â‚¹${order.subtotal}, Delivery: â‚¹${order.shippingFee || 0}, Discount: â‚¹${order.discount || 0})`,
      ``,
      `ðŸ‘¤ CUSTOMER DETAILS:`,
      `â€¢ Name: ${customerName}`,
      `â€¢ Phone: ${customerPhone}`,
      `â€¢ Email: ${customerEmail}`,
      `â€¢ Address: ${deliveryAddress}`,
      `â€¢ Landmark: ${landmark}`,
      `â€¢ Google Maps Pin: ${mapsLink}`,
      ``,
      `ðŸ“¦ ORDERED ITEMS:`,
      itemsSummary,
      ``,
      isCod ? `âš ï¸ ACTION REQUIRED: COLLECT â‚¹${order.total} CASH ON DELIVERY AT DOORSTEP` : `âœ… Online Payment Completed`,
    ].join("\n");

    const templateParams: Record<string, string> = {
      to_email: EMAILJS_CONFIG.TARGET_EMAIL,
      to_name: "MANOPAVANA Team",
      from_name: customerName,
      from_email: customerEmail !== "N/A" ? customerEmail : "orders@manopavana.com",
      reply_to: customerEmail !== "N/A" ? customerEmail : EMAILJS_CONFIG.TARGET_EMAIL,
      subject: `[${isCod ? "COD ORDER" : "PAID ORDER"}] #${order.orderRef} - â‚¹${order.total} by ${customerName}`,
      title: `Order #${order.orderRef} (${paymentMethodLabel})`,

      // Primary Message & Content fallbacks (the most common EmailJS variables)
      message: fullOrderText,
      content: fullOrderText,
      body: fullOrderText,
      email_body: fullOrderText,
      text: fullOrderText,
      details: fullOrderText,
      order_details: fullOrderText,
      order_summary: fullOrderText,
      orderSummary: fullOrderText,
      summary: fullOrderText,
      notes: fullOrderText,
      description: fullOrderText,
      my_message: fullOrderText,

      // Order Reference variations
      order_ref: order.orderRef,
      orderRef: order.orderRef,
      order_id: order.orderRef || order.id,
      orderId: order.orderRef || order.id,
      order_number: order.orderRef,
      id: order.orderRef || order.id,

      // Date variations
      order_date: formattedDate,
      date: formattedDate,
      time: formattedDate,

      // Customer Name variations
      customer_name: customerName,
      customerName: customerName,
      name: customerName,
      user_name: customerName,
      client_name: customerName,

      // Customer Phone variations
      customer_phone: customerPhone,
      customerPhone: customerPhone,
      phone: customerPhone,
      user_phone: customerPhone,
      contact: customerPhone,
      mobile: customerPhone,
      phone_number: customerPhone,

      // Email variations
      customer_email: customerEmail,
      email: customerEmail,
      user_email: customerEmail,

      // Address variations
      delivery_address: deliveryAddress,
      deliveryAddress: deliveryAddress,
      address: deliveryAddress,
      shipping_address: deliveryAddress,
      shippingAddress: deliveryAddress,
      street_address: addressLine,
      address_line1: addressLine,
      landmark: landmark,
      city: district,
      district: district,
      state: state,
      pincode: pincode,
      zip: pincode,
      zipcode: pincode,

      // Item summary variations
      items_summary: itemsSummary,
      itemsSummary: itemsSummary,
      items: itemsSummary,
      order_items: itemsSummary,
      item_list: itemsSummary,
      products: itemsSummary,
      cart: itemsSummary,

      // Pricing variations
      total_amount: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      totalAmount: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      total: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      amount: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      grand_total: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      price: `â‚¹${order.total}`,
      subtotal: `â‚¹${order.subtotal}`,
      discount: `â‚¹${order.discount || 0}`,
      shipping_fee: `â‚¹${order.shippingFee || 0}`,

      // Payment variations
      payment_method: paymentMethodLabel,
      paymentMethod: paymentMethodLabel,
      payment_mode: order.paymentMode || paymentMethodLabel,
      paymentMode: order.paymentMode || paymentMethodLabel,
      payment_status: order.paymentStatus || (isCod ? "COD_PENDING_HANDOVER" : "COMPLETED"),
      paymentStatus: order.paymentStatus || (isCod ? "COD_PENDING_HANDOVER" : "COMPLETED"),

      // Facility & Logistics
      is_bangalore: order.shippingAddress?.isBangalore ? "Yes (Peenya Direct Doorstep)" : "Pan-India Route",
      maps_link: mapsLink,
      mapsLink: mapsLink,
      map_url: mapsLink,
      google_maps: mapsLink,
      tracking_number: order.trackingNumber || `MV-BLR-${Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000)}`,
      courier: order.courier || (order.shippingAddress?.isBangalore ? "MANOPAVANA Peenya Express" : "Delhivery Express"),
    };

    const res = await emailjs.send(
      EMAILJS_CONFIG.SERVICE_ID,
      EMAILJS_CONFIG.TEMPLATE_ID,
      templateParams,
      EMAILJS_CONFIG.PUBLIC_KEY
    );

    console.log("EmailJS order dispatch confirmation sent successfully:", res.status, res.text);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("EmailJS delivery notice:", error);
    return false;
  }
}

// Automatically scrolls to top on route change
function ScrollToTop() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();

  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }, [pathname]);

  return null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<
  { children: React.ReactNode },
  { hasError: boolean; error: Error | null }
> {
  constructor(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo) {
    console.warn("ErrorBoundary captured error:", error, errorInfo);
  }

  handleEmergencyReset = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.clear();
      sessionStorage.clear();
      indexedDB.deleteDatabase("firebaseLocalStorageDb");
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
    window.location.href = "/";
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex items-center justify-center p-6 bg-[#FAF6F0] text-[#24140A] text-center">
          <div className="max-w-md w-full p-8 bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl border border-[#D49A37]/40 space-y-5">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 mx-auto rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-[#E08F35]">
              <RefreshCw className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold font-serif text-[#5E3210]">
                Something interrupted this view
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#7A5E48]">
                A data format error occurred while rendering this section.
              </p>
              {this.state.error?.message && (
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-red-600 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg border border-red-200 break-all text-left">
                  {this.state.error.message}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
                  window.location.reload();
                }}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#5E3210] text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#43230A] transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-md flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <RefreshCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Try Refreshing View</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={this.handleEmergencyReset}
                className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-700 border border-red-200 text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-600" />
                <span>Emergency Sign Out & Return Home</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

export default function App() {
  const {
    currentUser,
    toastMessage,
    getOrder,
    clearCart,
    clearBuyNow,
    setOrderSuccessRef,
    setPaymentFailedOrder,
    showToast,
    cart,
    subtotal,
    discount,
    shippingFee,
    total,
    address,
    themeMode,
    isPreviewStorefront,
    togglePreviewStorefront,
    logoutUser,
    logoutAdmin,
  } = useStore();

  // REAL PAYMENT RETURN & VERIFICATION ROUTE HANDLER
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const orderRef = params.get("orderRef");
    if (!orderRef) return;

    // Clean up URL parameters immediately using replaceState
    window.history.replaceState({}, document.title, window.location.pathname);

    const targetRef = orderRef;

    async function verifyReturn(ref: string) {
      try {
        let matched: Order | null | undefined = await getOrderFromFirestore(ref);
        if (!matched) {
          matched = getOrder(ref) || null;
        }
        if (!matched) {
          try {
            const pendingRaw = localStorage.getItem("pending_order_data");
            if (pendingRaw) matched = JSON.parse(pendingRaw);
          } catch {
            // ignore
          }
        }

        const resp = await fetch(`/api?action=verify_order&orderRef=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`);
        const data = await resp.json();
        const code = data.code || "";
        const state = data.data?.state || data.paymentStatus || "";

        if (
          data.status === "SUCCESS" ||
          code === "PAYMENT_SUCCESS" ||
          state === "COMPLETED" ||
          state === "SUCCESS"
        ) {
          localStorage.removeItem("pending_order_ref");
          localStorage.removeItem("pending_order_timestamp");
          localStorage.removeItem("pending_order_data");

          await updateFirestoreOrderStatus(ref, {
            orderStatus: "Placed",
            paymentMode: "PHONEPE_PAID",
            paymentStatus: "COMPLETED",
          });

          if (matched) {
            await sendOrderConfirmationEmail({
              ...matched,
              orderStatus: "Placed",
              paymentMode: "PHONEPE_PAID",
              paymentStatus: "COMPLETED",
            });
          }

          if (matched?.isBuyNow) {
            clearBuyNow();
          } else {
            clearCart();
            clearBuyNow();
          }
          setOrderSuccessRef(ref);
          showToast("Payment verified online! Order placed.");
        } else {
          await updateFirestoreOrderStatus(ref, {
            orderStatus: "Payment Incomplete",
            paymentStatus: "FAILED",
          });

          if (matched && matched.items && matched.items.length > 0) {
            setPaymentFailedOrder(matched);
          } else {
            const fallbackItems = cart.length > 0 ? [...cart] : [];
            const fallbackSubtotal = fallbackItems.reduce((acc, i) => acc + i.price * i.quantity, 0);
            const fallbackTotal = Math.max(0, fallbackSubtotal - (discount || 0) + (shippingFee || 0));

            const fallbackOrder = {
              id: ref,
              orderRef: ref,
              date: "Just now",
              timestamp: Date.now(),
              timestampMs: Date.now(),
              items: fallbackItems,
              subtotal: fallbackSubtotal,
              discount: discount || 0,
              shippingFee: shippingFee || 0,
              total: fallbackTotal,
              paymentMethod: "phonepe" as const,
              paymentMode: "PHONEPE",
              paymentStatus: "FAILED",
              orderStatus: "Payment Incomplete" as const,
              shippingAddress: address,
            };
            if (fallbackOrder.items.length > 0) {
              setPaymentFailedOrder(fallbackOrder);
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Payment verification error:", err);
        let matched: Order | null | undefined = await getOrderFromFirestore(ref);
        if (!matched) matched = getOrder(ref) || null;
        if (matched && matched.items && matched.items.length > 0) {
          setPaymentFailedOrder(matched);
        }
      }
    }

    verifyReturn(targetRef);
  }, []);

  const isStoreAdmin = currentUser?.email?.toLowerCase().trim() === "manopavana@gmail.com";

  // Dedicated Admin Header with Escape & Logout Options
  if (isStoreAdmin && !isPreviewStorefront) {
    return (
      <div
        className={`w-full max-w-full overflow-x-hidden min-h-screen flex flex-col font-['Outfit',sans-serif] box-border transition-colors duration-200 ${
          themeMode === "dark"
            ? "bg-[#140D09] text-[#FDF6EC]"
            : themeMode === "light"
            ? "bg-[#FAF7F2] text-[#24140A]"
            : "bg-[#FAF6F0] text-[#24140A]"
        }`}
      >
        <ScrollToTop />

        {/* Global Admin Navigation Bar to prevent ever getting trapped */}
        <header className="bg-[#2D1B10] text-[#FDF6EC] px-4 py-2.5 text-xs flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#D49A37]/40 sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-md">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
            <span className="font-bold text-amber-200">MANOPAVANA Master Admin Portal</span>
            <span className="text-[#C4B29E] hidden md:inline">({currentUser?.email})</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => togglePreviewStorefront(true)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#E08F35] hover:bg-[#c97a26] text-[#1F120A] text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1 shadow"
            >
              <Home className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Preview Storefront</span>
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={async () => {
                await logoutUser();
                logoutAdmin();
                window.location.href = "/";
              }}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1 shadow"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Sign Out</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        <ErrorBoundary>
          <main className="flex-1">
            <AdminPage />
          </main>
        </ErrorBoundary>

        {/* Floating Global Toast Notification */}
        {toastMessage && (
          <div className="fixed top-20 right-5 z-50 flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#1C110B]/95 text-[#FDF6EC] text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xl border border-[#D49A37]/50 backdrop-blur-md animate-bounce">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#F5BC51] shrink-0" />
            <span>{toastMessage}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`w-full max-w-full overflow-x-hidden min-h-screen flex flex-col font-['Outfit',sans-serif] box-border transition-colors duration-200 ${
        themeMode === "dark"
          ? "bg-[#140D09] text-[#FDF6EC]"
          : themeMode === "light"
          ? "bg-[#FAF7F2] text-[#24140A]"
          : "bg-[#FAF6F0] text-[#24140A]"
      }`}
    >
      <ScrollToTop />

      {/* Admin Customer Storefront Preview Sticky Top Bar */}
      {isStoreAdmin && isPreviewStorefront && (
        <div className="bg-[#2D1B10] text-[#FDF6EC] px-4 py-2.5 text-xs flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#D49A37]/40 sticky top-0 z-50 shadow-md">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
            <span className="font-bold">Storefront Customer Preview</span>
            <span className="text-[#C4B29E] hidden md:inline">
              (Viewing storefront as customer â€¢ Logged in as {currentUser?.email})
            </span>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => togglePreviewStorefront(false)}
            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#E08F35] hover:bg-[#c97a26] text-[#1F120A] text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow"
          >
            <span>âš™ï¸ Return to Master CMS</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <PendingOrderBanner />
      <Navbar />

      <ErrorBoundary>
        <main className="w-full max-w-full overflow-x-hidden flex-1 box-border">
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
            <Route path="/product/:id" element={<ProductPage />} />
            <Route path="/cart" element={<CartPage />} />
            <Route path="/checkout" element={<CheckoutPage />} />
            <Route path="/account" element={<AccountPage />} />
            <Route
              path="/admin"
              element={
                isStoreAdmin ? (
                  <AdminPage />
                ) : (
                  <Navigate to="/" replace />
                )
              }
            />
          </Routes>
        </main>
      </ErrorBoundary>

      <Footer />
      <AudioPlayer />
      <FreeShippingNudge />
      <ErrorBoundary>
        <LocationModal />
      </ErrorBoundary>
      <LoyaltyQuizModal />
      <LoginModal />
      <PaymentFailedModal />
      <OrderSuccessModal />

      {/* Floating Global Toast Notification */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed top-20 right-5 z-50 flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#1C110B]/95 text-[#FDF6EC] text-xs font-semibold shadow-2xl border border-[#D49A37]/50 backdrop-blur-md animate-bounce">
          <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#F5BC51] shrink-0" />
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  getFirestore,
  doc,
  setDoc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  collection,
  query,
  where,
  onSnapshot,
  orderBy,
  deleteDoc,
  serverTimestamp,
  increment,
  arrayUnion,
  getDocFromServer,
} from "firebase/firestore";
import firebaseConfig from "../firebase-applet-config.json";
import { CustomerReview, Order, StoreCMSConfig } from "./types";

// Initialize Firebase safely with provisioned Database ID
const app = !getApps().length ? initializeApp(firebaseConfig) : getApp();
export const auth = getAuth(app);
export const db = getFirestore(app, (firebaseConfig as any).firestoreDatabaseId);
export const googleProvider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
googleProvider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: "select_account" });

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = "create",
  UPDATE = "update",
  DELETE = "delete",
  LIST = "list",
  GET = "get",
  WRITE = "write",
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function handleFirestoreError(error: unknown, operationType: OperationType, path: string | null) {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo:
        auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map((p) => ({
          providerId: p.providerId,
          email: p.email,
        })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.warn("Firestore Error: ", JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

// Test live database connectivity at boot
async function testFirestoreConnection() {
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, "test", "connection"));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message.includes("the client is offline")) {
      console.warn("Firestore offline notice. Please verify network.");
    }
  }
}
testFirestoreConnection();

/**
 * Clean phone number to 10 digits
 */
export function extractCleanPhone(input?: string): string {
  if (!input) return "";
  const digitsOnly = input.replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (digitsOnly.length === 10) return digitsOnly;
  if (digitsOnly.length === 12 && digitsOnly.startsWith("91")) return digitsOnly.slice(2);
  return digitsOnly.slice(-10);
}

/**
 * Format 10-digit phone number into a valid Firebase email
 */
export function formatLoginIdentifier(input: string): string {
  const cleaned = input.trim();
  const digits = extractCleanPhone(cleaned);
  if (digits.length === 10 && !cleaned.includes("@")) {
    return `${digits}@phone.manopavana.com`;
  }
  return cleaned.toLowerCase();
}

/**
 * 1-Click Google Sign-In
 */
export async function signInWithGoogle() {
  const result = await signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
  if (result.user) {
    await syncCustomerDoc(result.user, {
      name: result.user.displayName || "Google Customer",
      email: result.user.email || undefined,
      phone: result.user.phoneNumber || undefined,
    });
  }
  return result.user;
}

/**
 * Phone or Email Login with Automatic Registration fallback
 */
export async function signInWithPhoneOrEmail(identifier: string, password = "manopavana_default_pwd") {
  const formattedEmail = formatLoginIdentifier(identifier);
  const cleanPhone = extractCleanPhone(identifier);

  try {
    const cred = await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, formattedEmail, password);
    if (cred.user) {
      await syncCustomerDoc(cred.user, {
        email: !formattedEmail.endsWith("@phone.manopavana.com") ? formattedEmail : undefined,
        phone: cleanPhone || undefined,
      });
    }
    return cred.user;
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const error = err as { code?: string; message?: string };
    // If user does not exist yet, automatically create account
    if (
      error.code === "auth/user-not-found" ||
      error.code === "auth/invalid-credential" ||
      error.code === "auth/invalid-login-credentials"
    ) {
      const newCred = await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, formattedEmail, password);
      if (newCred.user) {
        await syncCustomerDoc(newCred.user, {
          email: !formattedEmail.endsWith("@phone.manopavana.com") ? formattedEmail : undefined,
          phone: cleanPhone || undefined,
        });
      }
      return newCred.user;
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Sign out current user
 */
export async function logoutUser() {
  await signOut(auth);
}

/**
 * Sync & Deduplicate customer details in Firestore 'customers' collection:
 * Merges profile, savedAddress, and cart seamlessly.
 */
export async function syncCustomerDoc(
  user: FirebaseUser,
  extra?: { name?: string; phone?: string; email?: string }
) {
  try {
    const userEmail = (extra?.email || user.email || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    const isSyntheticPhoneEmail = userEmail.endsWith("@phone.manopavana.com");
    const canonicalEmail = isSyntheticPhoneEmail ? "" : userEmail;

    const rawPhone = extra?.phone || user.phoneNumber || "";
    const cleanPhone = extractCleanPhone(rawPhone) || (isSyntheticPhoneEmail ? userEmail.split("@")[0] : "");

    const customerRef = doc(db, "customers", user.uid);
    const snap = await getDoc(customerRef);

    let existingData: Record<string, unknown> = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : {};
    let matchedDocRef = customerRef;

    // Search for existing customer profile by email or phone
    const customersCol = collection(db, "customers");

    if (canonicalEmail) {
      try {
        const qEmail = query(customersCol, where("email", "==", canonicalEmail));
        const emailSnap = await getDocs(qEmail);
        if (!emailSnap.empty) {
          const matchDoc = emailSnap.docs[0];
          matchedDocRef = matchDoc.ref;
          existingData = { ...matchDoc.data(), ...existingData };
        }
      } catch (e) {
        handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, "customers");
      }
    }

    if (cleanPhone && cleanPhone.length === 10) {
      try {
        const qPhone = query(customersCol, where("phone", "==", cleanPhone));
        const phoneSnap = await getDocs(qPhone);
        if (!phoneSnap.empty) {
          const matchDoc = phoneSnap.docs[0];
          matchedDocRef = matchDoc.ref;
          existingData = { ...matchDoc.data(), ...existingData };
        }
      } catch (e) {
        handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, "customers");
      }
    }

    const resolvedName =
      (extra?.name && extra.name !== "Valued Customer" && extra.name !== "Google Customer" ? extra.name : "") ||
      (user.displayName && user.displayName !== "Customer" ? user.displayName : "") ||
      (existingData.name as string) ||
      "";

    const resolvedEmail = canonicalEmail || (existingData.email as string) || "";
    const resolvedPhone = cleanPhone || (existingData.phone as string) || "";

    const mergedPayload: Record<string, unknown> = {
      uid: user.uid,
      uids: arrayUnion(user.uid),
      name: resolvedName,
      email: resolvedEmail,
      phone: resolvedPhone,
      lastLogin: serverTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    };

    if (!existingData.createdAt) {
      mergedPayload.createdAt = serverTimestamp();
      mergedPayload.totalOrders = 0;
      mergedPayload.totalSpent = 0;
    }

    // Preserve existing savedAddress and cart if already on file
    if (existingData.savedAddress) {
      mergedPayload.savedAddress = existingData.savedAddress;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(existingData.cart)) {
      mergedPayload.cart = existingData.cart;
    }

    const cleanPayload = cleanFirestoreObject(mergedPayload);
    await setDoc(matchedDocRef, cleanPayload, { merge: true });
    if (matchedDocRef.id !== user.uid) {
      await setDoc(customerRef, cleanPayload, { merge: true });
    }

    // Link any past guest orders matching this phone or email to this user UID
    linkPastOrdersToCustomer(user.uid, resolvedPhone, resolvedEmail);

    return { ...existingData, ...cleanPayload };
  } catch (e) {
    handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.WRITE, `customers/${user.uid}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Link past guest orders matching phone or email to the customer's UID
 */
async function linkPastOrdersToCustomer(userUid: string, phone: string, email: string) {
  try {
    const ordersCol = collection(db, "orders");
    const queries = [];
    if (phone && phone.length === 10) {
      queries.push(query(ordersCol, where("customerPhone", "==", phone)));
    }
    if (email && email.includes("@")) {
      queries.push(query(ordersCol, where("customerEmail", "==", email.toLowerCase())));
    }

    for (const q of queries) {
      const snap = await getDocs(q);
      for (const d of snap.docs) {
        const orderData = d.data();
        if (!orderData.customerUid || orderData.customerUid !== userUid) {
          await setDoc(d.ref, { customerUid: userUid, updatedAt: serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.UPDATE, "orders");
  }
}

/**
 * Sanitize object for Firestore:
 * Preserves Date and Firestore FieldValues (serverTimestamp, increment, arrayUnion)
 * Strips undefined values to prevent SDK errors.
 */
export function cleanFirestoreObject(obj: any): any {
  if (obj === undefined) return null;
  if (obj === null) return null;
  if (typeof obj !== "object") return obj;
  if (obj instanceof Date) return obj;

  // Preserve Firestore sentinel field values
  if (
    (obj as any)._methodName ||
    (obj as any).constructor?.name === "FieldValueImpl" ||
    (obj as any)._delegate
  ) {
    return obj;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    return obj.map(cleanFirestoreObject);
  }

  const cleaned: Record<string, any> = {};
  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(obj)) {
    if (val === undefined) {
      continue;
    } else if (val && typeof val === "object") {
      cleaned[key] = cleanFirestoreObject(val);
    } else {
      cleaned[key] = val;
    }
  }
  return cleaned;
}

/**
 * Fetch an order directly by orderRef from Firestore
 */
export async function getOrderFromFirestore(orderRef: string): Promise<Order | null> {
  if (!orderRef) return null;
  try {
    const orderDocRef = doc(db, "orders", orderRef);
    const snap = await getDoc(orderDocRef);
    if (snap.exists()) {
      return snap.data() as Order;
    }
    return null;
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.GET, `orders/${orderRef}`);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Save order document in Firestore 'orders' collection
 */
export async function saveOrderToFirestore(order: Order, userUid?: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const orderDocRef = doc(db, "orders", order.orderRef);
    const cleanPhone = extractCleanPhone(order.shippingAddress?.phone);
    const cleanEmail = (order.shippingAddress?.email || "").toLowerCase().trim();

    const uid = userUid || order.customerUid || (order as any).firebaseUid || null;
    const rawPayload = {
      ...order,
      customerUid: uid,
      firebaseUid: uid,
      customerPhone: cleanPhone || null,
      customerEmail: cleanEmail || null,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    };

    const sanitizedPayload = cleanFirestoreObject(rawPayload);

    // 5-second race timeout to guarantee non-hanging behavior
    await Promise.race([
      setDoc(orderDocRef, sanitizedPayload, { merge: true }),
      new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("Firestore save timeout")), 5000)),
    ]);

    if (uid) {
      const customerRef = doc(db, "customers", uid);
      setDoc(
        customerRef,
        {
          totalOrders: increment(1),
          totalSpent: increment(order.total || 0),
          lastOrderRef: order.orderRef,
          lastOrderAt: serverTimestamp(),
        },
        { merge: true }
      ).catch(() => {});
    }
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.WRITE, `orders/${order.orderRef}`);
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Update order status or payment status in Firestore
 */
export async function updateFirestoreOrderStatus(
  orderRef: string,
  updates: Partial<Order>
): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const orderDocRef = doc(db, "orders", orderRef);
    const cleanUpdates = cleanFirestoreObject({
      ...updates,
      updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });

    await Promise.race([
      setDoc(orderDocRef, cleanUpdates, { merge: true }),
      new Promise((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("Firestore update timeout")), 5000)),
    ]);
    return true;
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.UPDATE, `orders/${orderRef}`);
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Listen to orders for a specific user in real-time.
 */
export function subscribeUserOrders(
  userUid: string | undefined,
  phone: string | undefined,
  email: string | undefined,
  callback: (orders: Order[]) => void
) {
  try {
    const ordersCol = collection(db, "orders");
    const cleanEmail = (email || "").toLowerCase().trim();
    const unsubs: (() => void)[] = [];
    const aggregatedMap = new Map<string, Order>();

    const emitAggregated = () => {
      const list = Array.from(aggregatedMap.values()).sort(
        (a, b) => (b.timestamp || b.timestampMs || 0) - (a.timestamp || a.timestampMs || 0)
      );
      callback(list);
    };

    if (userUid) {
      const qUid = query(ordersCol, where("customerUid", "==", userUid));
      const unsub1 = onSnapshot(
        qUid,
        (snapshot) => {
          snapshot.forEach((d) => {
            const data = d.data() as Order;
            if (!data.customerUid || data.customerUid === userUid || (data as any).firebaseUid === userUid) {
              aggregatedMap.set(d.id, data);
            }
          });
          emitAggregated();
        },
        (error) => {
          handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, "orders");
        }
      );
      unsubs.push(unsub1);
    }

    if (cleanEmail && cleanEmail.includes("@") && !cleanEmail.endsWith("@phone.manopavana.com")) {
      const qEmail = query(ordersCol, where("customerEmail", "==", cleanEmail));
      const unsubEmail = onSnapshot(
        qEmail,
        (snapshot) => {
          snapshot.forEach((d) => {
            const data = d.data() as Order;
            if (!data.customerUid || data.customerUid === userUid) {
              aggregatedMap.set(d.id, data);
            }
          });
          emitAggregated();
        },
        (error) => {
          handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, "orders");
        }
      );
      unsubs.push(unsubEmail);
    }

    return () => {
      unsubs.forEach((u) => u());
    };
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.LIST, "orders");
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Listen to all reviews from Firestore 'reviews' collection
 */
export function subscribeReviews(callback: (reviews: CustomerReview[]) => void) {
  try {
    const reviewsCol = collection(db, "reviews");
    const q = query(reviewsCol, orderBy("createdAt", "desc"));
    return onSnapshot(
      q,
      (snapshot) => {
        const list: CustomerReview[] = [];
        snapshot.forEach((d) => {
          list.push({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() as Omit<CustomerReview, "id">) });
        });
        callback(list);
      },
      (error) => {
        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.LIST, "reviews");
      }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.LIST, "reviews");
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Add a new customer review to Firestore
 */
export async function addReviewToFirestore(review: Omit<CustomerReview, "id">) {
  try {
    const reviewsCol = collection(db, "reviews");
    const newDoc = doc(reviewsCol);
    await setDoc(newDoc, {
      ...review,
      createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
    });
    return newDoc.id;
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.WRITE, "reviews");
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Delete a review from Firestore
 */
export async function deleteReviewFromFirestore(reviewId: string) {
  try {
    const reviewDocRef = doc(db, "reviews", reviewId);
    await deleteDoc(reviewDocRef);
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.DELETE, `reviews/${reviewId}`);
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * Save store CMS configuration to Firestore document 'settings/store_config'
 */
export async function saveStoreConfigToFirestore(config: StoreCMSConfig) {
  try {
    const configDocRef = doc(db, "settings", "store_config");
    await setDoc(
      configDocRef,
      {
        ...config,
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
      },
      { merge: true }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.WRITE, "settings/store_config");
  }
}

/**
 * Subscribe to store CMS configuration in Firestore 'settings/store_config'
 */
export function subscribeStoreConfig(callback: (config: StoreCMSConfig) => void) {
  try {
    const configDocRef = doc(db, "settings", "store_config");
    return onSnapshot(
      configDocRef,
      (docSnap) => {
        if (docSnap.exists()) {
          callback(docSnap.data() as StoreCMSConfig);
        }
      },
      (error) => {
        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.GET, "settings/store_config");
      }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.GET, "settings/store_config");
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Listen to all orders for admin dispatch queue
 */
export function subscribeAllOrders(callback: (orders: Order[]) => void) {
  try {
    const ordersCol = collection(db, "orders");
    const q = query(ordersCol, orderBy("createdAt", "desc"));
    return onSnapshot(
      q,
      (snapshot) => {
        const list: Order[] = [];
        snapshot.forEach((d) => {
          list.push(d.data() as Order);
        });
        callback(list);
      },
      (error) => {
        handleFirestoreError(error, OperationType.LIST, "orders");
        // Fallback without orderBy
        return onSnapshot(ordersCol, (snapshot) => {
          const list: Order[] = [];
          snapshot.forEach((d) => {
            list.push(d.data() as Order);
          });
          callback(list);
        });
      }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    handleFirestoreError(err, OperationType.LIST, "orders");
    return () => {};
  }
}

  };

  // Editable A-to-Z Legal Policies
  policies?: {

  productDetails?: Record<string, ProductDetailOverride>;
